Auto-generated by (fingerprint-index) -- a PROTOTYPE retrieval view. Each installed result is bucketed by the depth-3 structural fingerprint of its conclusion: head functor + immediate argument heads, recursively, with · for schema variables and numerals. Both sides of a top =/IFF are kept, so the key is the redex skeleton the matcher fires on. 4710 results, 3550 buckets (3023 singletons); 2132 -rev companions folded into their base (tagged (±)).
